> [!NOTE]
> This is a beta release. Details on this page may change before the stable release is published.

Read the [ESPHome 2026.11.0 release notes](/blog/2026/11/18/esphome-2026-11/) on the blog for the release
overview, feature highlights, upgrade checklist, and breaking changes.

{/* markdownlint-disable MD013 */}

## Full list of changes

### New Features

- [spd2010] Add touchscreen platform [esphome#19057](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/19057) by [@grischard](https://github.com/grischard) (new-component) (new-feature) (new-platform)
- [remote_base] Add Onkyo RI Wired Serial Support [esphome#18595](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/18595) by [@c0nsumer](https://github.com/c0nsumer) (new-feature)
- [gpio][esp32][uart] Add option to hold gpio during (deep)sleep and reboot (3/3) [esphome#18964](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/18964) by [@luar123](https://github.com/luar123) (new-feature)
- [mitsubishi] Add fahrenheit_compatibility mode [esphome#18880](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/18880) by [@zigboi](https://github.com/zigboi) (new-feature)
- [mitsubishi] Control both vanes on multi-vane Mitsubishi minisplit heads [esphome#12609](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/12609) by [@zigboi](https://github.com/zigboi) (new-feature)
- [cm1106] Add ABC function [esphome#8894](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/8894) by [@CzBiX](https://github.com/CzBiX) (new-feature)
- [tca9548a] Allow separate I²C bus frequencies on each multiplexer port [esphome#10422](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/10422) by [@DAVe3283](https://github.com/DAVe3283) (new-feature)
- [zigbee] add support for SED on esp32 [esphome#20487](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/20487) by [@luar123](https://github.com/luar123) (new-feature)
- [feedback] Support for overshoot_duration [esphome#13186](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/13186) by [@AchilleGR](https://github.com/AchilleGR) (new-feature)
- [cli] Add line format error message option [esphome#20470](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/20470) by [@clydebarrow](https://github.com/clydebarrow) (new-feature)
- [toshiba] Add 'seiya' model support [esphome#17830](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/17830) by [@longerCZE](https://github.com/longerCZE) (new-feature)
- [toshiba] Add RAS-B10N3KV2 model support [esphome#17550](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/17550) by [@RuedigerMueller](https://github.com/RuedigerMueller) (new-feature)
- [espnow] Add support for ESPNow with ESP8266 [esphome#14407](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/14407) by [@minorOffense](https://github.com/minorOffense) (new-feature)
- [hub75] add set_rotation and get_rotation methods [esphome#13921](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/13921) by [@tjhorner](https://github.com/tjhorner) (new-feature)
- [daikin] Add presets and half-degree temperature steps [esphome#17891](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/17891) by [@opcod3](https://github.com/opcod3) (new-feature)
- [tuya] Add forced minute boundary time sync [esphome#18603](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/18603) by [@MayorBug](https://github.com/MayorBug) (new-feature)
- [systa_bus] Add text_sensor [esphome#20518](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/20518) by [@Mat931](https://github.com/Mat931) (new-feature) (new-platform)
- [tmp102] Add hardware configuration options [esphome#20063](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/20063) by [@techdregs](https://github.com/techdregs) (new-feature)
- [ufm01] Improve stale recovery, add diagnostics and clear flow action [esphome#18899](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/18899) by [@ljungqvist](https://github.com/ljungqvist) (new-feature) (new-platform)
- [pmsx003] add support for luftmy ld15 [esphome#15296](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/15296) by [@tuct](https://github.com/tuct) (new-feature)
- [lvgl] Migrate to 9.6.0 [esphome#20257](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/20257) by [@clydebarrow](https://github.com/clydebarrow) (new-feature)
- [pipsolar] Add QET/QLT total energy sensors [esphome#17347](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/17347) by [@mkhrulov](https://github.com/mkhrulov) (new-feature)
- [cse7761] Add new sensors [esphome#17531](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/17531) by [@fornellas](https://github.com/fornellas) (new-feature)
- [emontx] Add pause/resume actions to let another component take over the UART [esphome#18756](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/18756) by [@FredM67](https://github.com/FredM67) (new-feature)
- [mipi_dsi] Add JC1060P470-V2 [esphome#18695](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/18695) by [@mkucharek](https://github.com/mkucharek) (new-feature)
- [zigbee] Improve basic cluster and add manufacturer and app version setting [esphome#20519](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/20519) by [@luar123](https://github.com/luar123) (new-feature)
- [zigbee] add report action on esp32 [esphome#20488](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/20488) by [@luar123](https://github.com/luar123) (new-feature)
- [micro_wake_word] Add opt-in internal RAM placement [esphome#20529](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/20529) by [@mbrudka](https://github.com/mbrudka) (new-feature)
- [openthread] add mesh_local_eid openthread_info sensor [esphome#13924](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/13924) by [@mrene](https://github.com/mrene) (new-feature)
- [tmp102] Add comparator alert binary sensor [esphome#20540](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/20540) by [@techdregs](https://github.com/techdregs) (new-feature) (new-platform)
- [remote_base] Add optional suffix bits to KeeLoq after the HCS301 word [esphome#19891](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/19891) by [@Bascht74](https://github.com/Bascht74) (new-feature)
- [mipi_spi] Add LCDwiki ES3C35P model (ST77922 QSPI) [esphome#19011](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/19011) by [@jlmeredith](https://github.com/jlmeredith) (new-feature)
- [cse7761] Add current_input option for current transformer boards such as the Sonoff POW CT [esphome#7836](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/7836) by [@mick96](https://github.com/mick96) (new-feature)
- [lvgl] Add a color picker widget [esphome#17883](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/17883) by [@clydebarrow](https://github.com/clydebarrow) (new-feature)

### Beta Changes

- [esp32_ble] Fix build on Windows when Bluedroid's private headers are not reachable [esphome#22257](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22257) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [ld2410][ld2412] Drop signal_strength device class from the gate threshold numbers [esphome#22259](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22259) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [core] Dump typed_schema types that have no keys of their own [esphome#22258](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22258) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [tfluna] Drop signal_strength device class from the signal strength sensor [esphome#22260](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22260) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [rp2040_pio_led_strip] Fail setup instead of panicking when no state machine or DMA channel is free [esphome#22152](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22152) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [rp2040_pio_led_strip] Size the DMA channel arrays for RP2350 [esphome#22153](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22153) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [rp2040_pio_led_strip] Compute the PIO clock divider with its fraction [esphome#22265](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22265) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [mdns] Bump espressif/mdns to 1.15.0 [esphome#22267](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22267) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [logger] Report the RP2 crash record when a host opens the USB port [esphome#22266](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22266) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [sm2135] Fix light turning off when switching color modes [esphome#19438](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/19438) by [@ryan-ronnander](https://github.com/ryan-ronnander)
- [docker] Bump base image to 2026.11.0 [esphome#22275](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22275) by [@swoboda1337](https://github.com/swoboda1337)
- [mqtt] Cap incoming message size with max_payload_size [esphome#22278](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22278) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [core] Keep a trigger's only extra key in the language schema [esphome#21801](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21801) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [libretiny] Record abort() as a crash and reboot [esphome#22288](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22288) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [schema] Keep sibling extends when shrink inlines one schema [esphome#22287](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22287) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [wifi] Log why a join failed on RTL87xx [esphome#21751](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21751) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [wifi] Ignore the scan inside a join on RTL87xx [esphome#21749](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21749) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [wifi] Treat open networks as open in RTL87xx scan results [esphome#21750](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21750) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [web_server_base] Apply the web server's allowed origins check to every handler [esphome#21972](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21972) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco) (breaking-change)
- [wifi] Retry at once when a scan leaves no hidden network to try [esphome#21747](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21747) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [esp32_hosted] Bump esp_hosted to 3.1.0 and fix the host restart after a coprocessor OTA [esphome#22306](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22306) by [@swoboda1337](https://github.com/swoboda1337)
- [esp32_ble] Silence the PACKED redefined warning from Bluedroid's private headers [esphome#22296](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22296) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [nrf52] Download the pinned nrfutil launcher by its versioned file name [esphome#22314](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22314) by [@tomaszduda23](https://github.com/tomaszduda23)
- [sun] Pick the next sunrise or sunset from the right day for any longitude [esphome#22308](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22308) by [@swoboda1337](https://github.com/swoboda1337)
- [api] Let components give wizard entities a stable role [esphome#22295](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22295) by [@jesserockz](https://github.com/jesserockz)
- [mqtt] Keep retained messages from overflowing the ESP32 event queue at connect [esphome#22248](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22248) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [xiaomi_body_scale] Handle the S400 Pro 0x7FF no-weight marker [esphome#22317](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22317) by [@dckiller51](https://github.com/dckiller51)

- [wifi] Ignore the scan inside a join on RTL87xx [esphome#21749](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21749) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [wifi] Treat open networks as open in RTL87xx scan results [esphome#21750](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21750) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [web_server_base] Apply the web server's allowed origins check to every handler [esphome#21972](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21972) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco) (breaking-change)
- [wifi] Retry at once when a scan leaves no hidden network to try [esphome#21747](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/21747) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [esp32_hosted] Bump esp_hosted to 3.1.0 and fix the host restart after a coprocessor OTA [esphome#22306](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22306) by [@swoboda1337](https://github.com/swoboda1337)
- [esp32_ble] Silence the PACKED redefined warning from Bluedroid's private headers [esphome#22296](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22296) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [nrf52] Download the pinned nrfutil launcher by its versioned file name [esphome#22314](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22314) by [@tomaszduda23](https://github.com/tomaszduda23)
- [sun] Pick the next sunrise or sunset from the right day for any longitude [esphome#22308](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22308) by [@swoboda1337](https://github.com/swoboda1337)
- [api] Let components give wizard entities a stable role [esphome#22295](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22295) by [@jesserockz](https://github.com/jesserockz)
- [mqtt] Keep retained messages from overflowing the ESP32 event queue at connect [esphome#22248](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22248) by [@bdraco](https://github.com/bdraco)
- [xiaomi_body_scale] Handle the S400 Pro 0x7FF no-weight marker [esphome#22317](https://github.com/esphome/esphome/pull/22317) by [@dckiller51](https://github.com/dckiller51)
{/* ALL_CHANGES_END */}
